### [DES-OPERATE] Approved Plan B — PiPedal Tweaks and Processing Profiles

**Authorization:** Rix selected Plan B, accepts explicit T3K overwrite of PiPedal tone tweaks, wants minor adjustments at home/studio/live, and explicitly requires both System and Plugin settings, with existing host fields read-only. Previous delegated approval covers staging Spec → Design → Tasks and continued authorized implementation/E2E. Plans C/D remain unselected; no reverse writes or app modification. Earlier option/review records remain historical proposals; this section defines the activated contract.

**Settings ownership and storage:** add three fixed named profiles Home/Studio/Live, initially identical to the verified readable app defaults, otherwise existing service preferences or pinned defaults with source status. Never claim these initial copies are measured optimal configurations. Each profile holds the six processing preferences: bool multiCore, NAM default size 0/1, bool calibrateInput, calibration level within -60…60 dBu, bool osEnabled and integer osFactor 0…2. Store the profiles/active selection and the six active processor keys in one authoritative service-user `preferences.settings` file (private PiPedal profile JSON in a separate XML VALUE key). Native T3K ignores that extra metadata. Resolve effective service HOME/XDG, validate fixed file/ancestors and stable reads, reject links/DTD/entities/oversized XML, preserve unknown entries and ownership, retain exact private prewrite backups, write/fsync/publish atomically. Use a real XML parser such as libxml2 with network/entity resolution disabled; add/install its development dependency if required in the isolated Pi build. Do not infer a cross-file transaction.

Read the app's processing preferences only at a fixed sibling filename of configured preset roots and expose only the six typed keys. Set a narrow read ACL on that file when required, not account/UI preferences. No runtime browser-selected paths and no writes to the app store. Saving/activating a profile updates persisted defaults and **does not mutate the playing rig**. Explicit Apply or later plugin construction/load uses it. Multi-core/default NAM are constructor-owned machine globals, not per-rig overrides; state-preserved block sizes always remain unchanged until explicitly edited. Other currently running instances may retain earlier machine flags until rebuilt; the UI states this reload scope rather than claiming a live cross-process global update.

**Per-rig policy:** add optional `tone3000FollowDefaults` item metadata, false for old/missing fields and true for fresh library Adds; preserve it in native/browser state, saved banks, updates and snapshots through existing item identity. Follow applies the active profile's four calibration/oversampling session parameters at clean saved-rig load/startup and snapshot application, not every poll/state update. Dirty-session recovery preserves current unsaved values. Keep saved settings preserves those four values. Manual edits to those fields, including existing numeric Patch Set controls, select Keep saved settings unless the operator explicitly selects Follow/Apply. Fresh imports seed these four profile values rather than pinned defaults. Explicit library Update retains session overrides/policy and surrounding effects while replacing the exact tone/chain as previously defined. No automatic rewrite of other saved presets or old snapshots.

**Codec and block editing:** extend the bounded native codec with `SummarizeState` and `EditState`, using typed JSON DTOs with optional sparse fields. Return active preset ID/name, input mode, the four processing values, stereo/branch topology, and compact block summaries (stable block ID/lane/index, kind, title/category/format, active model ID, power, NAM normalization/size, normalized gains/mix and EQ). Never return embedded model bytes, URLs, tokens or arbitrary metadata. Requested size is 0/1; model capability/readiness remains unknown unless independently verified. IR size/normalization setters are rejected. Insert-only slots are displayed separately, not passed as NAM controls. Preserve every unedited raw property/tree/cache and unrelated LV2 state entries.

Validate finite normalized block fields in [0,1], power/normalize bools, unique edits/IDs, allowed lanes/types and Eq shapes/duplicates/tone identity. EQ has six pinned role-specific bands, 20–20000 Hz, gain ±15 dB, Q0.1–10: band 0 lowcut/lowshelf, bands 1–4 bell, band 5 highshelf/highcut. Missing legacy EQ uses native defaults for display; do not rewrite it until an explicit EQ edit. Root routing supports stereo/left/right/dual, but reject requests for stereo/dual when the pinned branched-stereo state would coerce them to left. No add/delete/reorder/swap editor is introduced.

**Runtime path and API:** `getTone3000Defaults` returns revision, activeProfile, profiles `{id,name,defaults}`, active defaults, optional appDefaults, sourceStatus and warnings. `setTone3000Defaults` takes expectedRevision, activeProfile and defaults, validates/backs up/publishes the single store, then returns refreshed state. `getTone3000CurrentState({instanceId})` freshly captures actual LV2 state with an explicit success/failure result and returns `{revision,instanceId,followDefaults,summary,hasSnapshotOverrides}`. `setTone3000CurrentState` takes instanceId/expectedRevision, sparse codec patch and optional followDefaults/applyDefaults; recapture and compare before mutation, reject stale/unknown input, clone current board preserving IDs/effects/MIDI/snapshots, apply patch/profile and use the existing safe host rebuild/dirty lifecycle. Normal PiPedal Save persists tweaks; requests never silently write the saved preset or source app file.

Add an optional force-reload ID set to the existing PluginHost reuse-map path; exclude only those IDs when explicitly applying constructor settings, preserving other effect instances. Do not falsify state bytes/revisions to defeat reuse. AudioHost needs an explicit state-capture success API because existing UpdatePluginState conflates unchanged with capture failure. State/profile revisions guard concurrent clients and actual audio/selection changes; a failed capture is a controlled error, not a summary of stale bytes. SET returns an **accepted** boolean rather than an immediate captured-state claim. Track the intended summary per changed instance; GET must observe its identities/settings/routing/processing fields (with numeric float tolerance and native insert padding) before clearing the apply guard. Normal Save/SaveAs also require that observation, avoiding capture of transient wrapper defaults. GET pending errors cause UI polling with editing disabled; no fixed delay is success/readiness evidence. Save/recall and metadata normalization use existing supported lifecycle behavior. A structural/block/EQ/profile Apply can briefly reload the selected T3K instance; it is **not a seamless-switch claim**, and no extra T3K/JUCE patch is planned. This bounded asynchronous-state safeguard was reviewed/reapproved under the same delegated Plan B scope before execution.

**Operator UI:** preserve Library and existing 43 controls. Add Blocks and Settings surfaces, with responsive cards/fields and explicit Apply for drafted block/EQ/routing edits. Provide Follow device defaults / Keep saved settings, effective current values, snapshot override notice, selected profile and Save defaults / Apply to this rig. Home/Studio/Live selection fills an editable draft; only Save defaults stages/activates it. Provide explicit Use T3K app defaults when readable. System device/driver, effective sample rate/block length, configured buffer count, input/output channels and enabled MIDI inputs come from existing PiPedal observable/RPC state and are **read-only**, with stopped/restarting/unknown status shown accurately. CPU/xruns reuse host status; no duplicate hardware setters, fake readiness or guessed safe quality thresholds. Preserve drafts during polls; discard stale responses after switching selected instance; serialize mutations and show actionable errors. The initial patch-based structural edit remains an explicit Apply to avoid reloading on every slider movement.

**Read-only chain presentation:** expand each actual TONE3000 node directly in PiPedal’s **main chain view** with the ordinary icon frames, spacing, labels, connectors and selection highlight. Rix rejected the large outlined group/header after live review: no enclosing border, background, extra header or duplicate inner input/output dots. Reuse the existing renderer in a nested read-only mode with an isolated display-only projection of freshly captured T3K summary blocks. Prepend one stock TONE3000 marker using the actual parent's plugin type and input-channel representation; it identifies ownership before the internal blocks. Clicking it selects the actual host ID and clears internal-block focus; it is display-only, not an extra audio processor. Include that ordinary cell in the measured layout, then trim/omit terminal cells so surrounding effects connect to the marker and final internal block/join. Preserve actual host position, real internal order/power/kind/title and supported branches; do not move the T3K instance ahead of existing host effects. Reading/error/empty fallbacks retain the existing real icon without an extra marker. The outer layout retains exactly one real host item/ID for the composite; local display IDs never enter outer host items, model setters or preset storage.

Each supported internal block icon provides view navigation: it selects the real parent, opens Blocks and focuses that captured block’s bounded settings card. The isolated renderer maps local display IDs to actual source block IDs; the selected block uses PiPedal’s ordinary highlight. This navigation never writes state; supported tweaks still require explicit Apply and normal Save. Internal dragging, double-click structural actions, context menus, add/delete/replace/reorder and live host subscriptions remain disabled because T3K owns structural authoring. Whole-plugin actions remain available through the existing selected-plugin toolbar; the inline children do not expose a whole-plugin drag handle. Compact fallback host icons retain ordinary host actions. Remove the duplicate lower-panel chain preview and omit empty slot cards; Blocks remains the editing surface.

**Main-chain capture boundary:** a functional wrapper around the existing main renderer owns a `tone3000Chains` map keyed only by real instance IDs. It observes host board/session and per-instance LV2-state changes, obtains summaries through the real `getTone3000CurrentState` capture service, and handles instances inside actual host splits. Request generations suppress responses from replaced boards/disconnected sessions; LV2 events during in-flight captures are coalesced into one follow-up read; unchanged captures do not broadcast again. Audio-configuration changes invalidate summaries and retry actual capture, without assuming WebSocket readiness implies audio readiness. Retry explicit pending-capture results; transient unavailable capture receives at most three additional actual reads (1/2/4 seconds), retaining an error if still unavailable. New state/audio events permit a fresh attempt; never treat elapsed time as success. Do not continuously capture every unselected instance. Summary errors remain visible and never fabricate routing. Pure topology tests cover stereo/branch/pass-through and rejection; browser verification separately checks placement, connectors, group selection and ordinary host actions. Gain fields display the pinned `(normalized−0.5)×48` dB conversion, and mix displays percent; API/storage remain normalized.

**Verification/rollout:** focused codec/profile/metadata/reload tests, static `/afx-check path` trace before gate completion, TypeScript/build, real native sources and service tests. Back up current owner selection/dirty state, exact current banks/config/preferences/frontend/package consistently; add scoped read ACL, build/package in isolated Pi checkout and install qualified artifacts. Test defaults staged without audio change, readonly System equivalence, current/old/fresh Follow policy, sparse block/EQ/routing changes, invalid/stale refusal, exact preserved assets/IDs/effects/MIDI/snapshots, Save/recall/restart, explicit T3K overwrite and narrow browser UI. Use disposable copies and restore owner state without overwriting concurrent edits; never erase new owner library entries. Measure reload gaps where possible, retain prior broad-test failures, and leave physical/listening/rehearsal/phone acceptance separate when unobserved.

**First slice: live status.** Extend existing audio status with truthful actual driver/device and fallback information obtained from the selected running driver, not inferred from requested settings. Preserve existing clients through optional additive fields. A small standard PiPedal status surface uses bounded read-only polling, no overlapping requests, ignores obsolete completions and clears stale status on disconnect. No automatic device changes in this UI task; late-interface recovery is a separately tested [7.9] backend task.

**First slice: readable controls.** Use exact pinned native formulas for input/output levels, balance, signed offset, crossover frequency, wobble, pan and existing physical-unit fields. Presentation must preserve actual numeric property values, ranges, choices, saved state and subscriptions. Use full known plugin property URIs and guard normalized-range assumptions. Unknown properties retain stock rendering. Readouts and numeric editing must agree; never label a raw editable normalized number as dB or milliseconds. Clamp physical entry before nonlinear conversion; negative frequency input must not become NaN. Default-reset gestures on patch controls use an optional callback into the raw patch setter; ordinary port-control reset retains its existing path. Pure endpoint/centre/roundtrip tests and unchanged raw-write checks precede rollout. Linked-pan/solo and effective capability are explicit later completion criteria, not achieved by a display-only formatter.

**Four-switch preparation.** Build on existing banks/PC and Performance view. Create copies in a new named performance bank in A/B/C/D order, preserving source IDs/order/content and surrounding effects. Server-side validation/atomic publication is needed because existing generic multi-copy iterates source order and cannot guarantee requested switch order. Existing MIDI Learn only forwards notes/CC; observing PC needs an opt-in monitor that does not change old listeners, synthesize messages, consume routing, or bypass host channel filtering. Distinguish observed MIDI, actual saved preset selection and audible success. Duplicate/wrong-channel/CC-only presses and stale bank/order changes must not become passes.

**Soundcheck and level comparison.** Native Auto Balance arms approximately two seconds of non-silent internal-chain energy measurement and applies outputBalance on the message thread; it is not across-preset matching. Auto Align runs an internal sweep with output temporarily muted and updates delay/polarity only from a trustworthy result. These are native methods, not existing numeric LV2 properties. A new versioned worker/message-thread-safe bridge must expose supported start/cancel/poll/result, enforce current instance/revision ownership and disconnect/timeouts, and produce validated persistent state; no host pointer casting or work on the real-time thread. Across-preset matching uses the same controlled phrase/measurement conditions and output trim only, presents measured evidence and proposed relative trims/lead boost, and applies once explicitly. No continuous gain riding or hidden changes during a set. Refine the concrete bridge/meter/storage protocol before [7.5]/[7.6] code.

**Portable rigs and repeatable auditions.** PiPedal already writes bundles with plugin metadata/media; extend validation and preview rather than introduce another archive. Embedded T3K asset presence is separate from real model readiness. DI capture/replay is bounded by duration/storage, uses an explicitly selected dry input and avoids feedback/double monitoring; audio remains owned by PiPedal, with no concurrent standalone interface ownership. Define actual routing, recording container, lifecycle and safe thread handoff here before implementation. Import/update remains one-way, with structural T3K authoring unchanged. Exact supported version/patch digest and dependency checks accompany portable imports and candidate builds.

**Qualification order.** [7.1]–[7.3] run locally first, then Linux host/browser/phone tests. [7.4] verifies real four-PC recall. [7.5]–[7.8] are later audio feature phases with focused thread/asset/state tests and actual listening. [7.9] verifies delayed USB and device-first cold boots. [7.10] builds pinned v0.0.12 with the existing readback correction and qualifies rollback, buffer/oversampling performance and a sustained rehearsal before updating the gig build. No measured latency, seamless T3K morphing or globally unique feature claim is made from discovery.

#### [DES-STAGE-USB] Late Configured-Device Recovery

Extend existing model RestartAudio lifecycle, not a driver-handle swap. A single generation-guarded dispatcher callback arms only after successful null fallback for valid non-null requested settings. Probe sparsely (2/4/8/15/30 seconds, then every30 seconds), with one pending callback and at most three opens per stable device-attachment episode. No repeated opens while absent or after the episode's attempt budget; observing both configured endpoints absent and then present permits a new bounded episode. This bounded work can watch indefinitely while fallback persists, so a device connected after five minutes can still recover. Exact configured directional endpoints, including busy-but-present endpoints, are required; never pick another interface or write settings.

Before each attempt recheck generation/settings/host/fallback. Drop the model lock before the established RestartAudio call; reload through normal plugin/subscription/channel lifecycle. Best-effort per-item capture retains dirty state and pending T3K intended state; no blind strict Sync or saved-bank overwrite. Real success, explicit settings/routing changes, intentional null selection and Close cancel recovery. Existing abnormal-stream retries stay separate. Enumeration can probe PCM endpoints, so it is sparse rather than the one-second status poll. Tests cover absence/late appearance, exact input/output matching, busy endpoints, budgets, stale generations, config/close cancellation and preserved rig/settings.

Serialize RestartAudio, Close and explicit audio configuration/routing entry points through a dedicated recursive lifecycle mutex, always acquired before the model mutex. Generation checking alone cannot cover the unlocked check-to-open interval. Existing AudioHost Close still occurs without the model mutex to avoid reader-thread deadlock; lifecycle serialization prevents an obsolete retry from reopening audio after intentional shutdown or a new configuration.

#### [DES-STAGE-BRIDGE] Native Soundcheck and Effective Controls

Add a versioned T3K-only LV2 extension `https://www.tone3000.com/plugin#pipedal-stage-v1` with bounded non-RT submit/read functions and request tokens. The wrapper validates/dynamic-casts a small declared processor bridge interface; PiPedal queries extension data on a verified T3K instance rather than casting host pointers to a foreign processor. A hash-pinned source overlay supplies the interface, processor implementation and wrapper hook after the existing JUCE readback correction.

Submit queues work on JUCE's existing message thread. One bounded request/measurement is active per instance; shutdown, obsolete token, cancellation and deadlines invalidate callbacks through retained shared lifetime state. No allocations/JSON/FFT or host parameter gestures run on the real-time thread. Native Auto Balance/Auto Align methods and message-thread polling own their existing measurement/mute/result semantics. Autonomous bounded timer polling completes/cancels a probe even if the browser stops polling; client/rig changes cancel server ownership. Completion captures real plugin state, marks the rig dirty and leaves normal explicit Save responsible for persistence. Unsupported old binaries return a capability error, not a pretend measurement.

Read-only runtime results whitelist actual loaded/loading/failed block status, model calibration metadata and actual supported/effective A2 information where available. Absent evidence stays unknown. Linked pan and mutually exclusive solo changes use native semantics with atomic whole edits; no delete/replace/reorder. Auto Balance is offered only with two live chains and measured non-silent playing. Mono rigs are reported not applicable, not given a invented trim. Auto Align explicitly warns that its native probe briefly mutes output.

The host also schedules one generation/token-guarded completion poll per active request on its existing dispatcher; native completion is captured and dirtied even when a connected browser stops polling. Reload, client cleanup and Close cancel ownership/timers. Bounded session receipts retain at most 32 valid played measurements across rig loads, expire on disconnect, and identify the exact saved board revision. `applyTone3000Level` consumes a receipt once, rereads and hashes the saved board, refuses dirty active targets, and applies only normalized outputLevel. Proposals are limited to ±12 dB, the native output range and at least 1 dB predicted peak headroom; errors are reported rather than silently clamped. The default comparison target is the quietest valid measured RMS with zero lead boost. Ordinary Save is required before measuring. This meter is after the T3K instance and before subsequent PiPedal effects; it is not a whole-board/FRFR loudness meter.

For [7.6], a bounded optional output-energy meter accumulates real post-plugin output only while armed, with finite/peak/clipping/silence/time/sample checks and message-thread results. Compare measurements of the same user-played phrase and expose the measured RMS plus proposed output-only trims. Apply once explicitly to identified/revision-checked rigs, with zero lead boost for these six Rx rhythms; retain input/calibration/drive and existing trims until valid approved measurements exist. This does not implement deferred DI recording/replay and cannot promise equal perceived loudness from RMS alone. No injected noise is used to manufacture a valid owner Auto Balance result.

### [DES-CONTROL-SCOPE] Rig, Device and Comparison Scope — 2026-10-07T21:18:54.000Z

Owner requests explicit scope after Soundcheck appeared global. Presentation-only clarification; no action/storage/lifecycle changes. Spec scope [FR-4]/[FR-12]/[FR-21] unchanged; focused Design → Tasks reviewed/reapproved under standing delegated authorization.

- Blocks, Tone, Stereo, Pitch and unrecognized live controls affect the current TONE3000 instance in the current PiPedal rig. Block drafts require Apply followed by normal PiPedal Save; direct live controls require Save to persist.
- Soundcheck is a mixed-scope tab: engine state, Auto Balance/Align and solo are current-instance tools. Successful native changes require normal Save; Auto Align can briefly mute. A three-second output measurement is taken from the current instance before later effects, never from the speaker/room.
- Comparison measurements span presets/instances only within this browser's connected session, survive rig/view switching, and expire on page reload/disconnection. They are not saved device-global settings or shared measurements between browsers. The quietest valid measurement defines the base target. Existing boost is added to that target for each row applied, not automatically reserved for a lead rig; label it Comparison boost with a plain helper. Each explicit Apply saves only the identified row's rig/instance trim; an active target can reload. It does not batch-change all presets, and revision/headroom guards remain authoritative.
- Settings live calibration/oversampling controls and captured processing values are per rig. Keep saved settings preserves them; Follow device defaults uses active calibration/oversampling defaults on later clean recall. Input routing and existing block A2 sizes remain rig-specific.

**[7.23] Soundcheck cancellation and board ownership:** source review identifies completed mutating jobs that can be lost when audio closes before cancellation/capture, or can overwrite a newly selected snapshot when late cancellation captures the previous runtime into the replacement model board. Reproduce with real codec/state/intent comparison and extracted actual production lifecycle methods; do not infer audible behavior from adapters. Cancellation must first submit native CANCEL, then inspect the retained terminal result: a completion that wins before cancellation remains capturable; cancellation that wins must prevent subsequent mutation. Extend cancellation with explicit retained-current-state policy. Capture completed work only against the still-owned current board before restart/ordinary replacement/disconnect; a fallback cleanup after a desired board is assigned must retire jobs without capturing old runtime into it. Cancel/capture before snapshot candidate construction, generic board replacement, fresh current-state revision/clone and audio Close, while keeping the model lock out of driver Close/join. Intentional structural replacements retire jobs without recapturing into supplied stale clones. Existing preset loads that already cancel before identity/storage changes retain that order; audit bank/plugin-preset paths too. Preserve accepted pending intents, ordinary dirty lifecycle and selected snapshot intent. No write to saved owner presets, no automatic trim, no native DSP/bridge ABI changes or new snapshot-save semantics. Independently compare the captured state to the last-loaded graph item, and invalidate the baseline on changed T3K state notifications too; the latter covers direct knob/Patch Set recall without an outstanding Stage job. Retain ordinary non-T3K notification behavior. Capture matching the loaded graph baseline must preserve the fast path. ### [DES-ROLLOUT] Rollout, Gates, and Recovery

Progress Gate 0 → Gate 1 → Gate 2 → Gate 3 → Gate 4 → Gate 5. Both feature-handshake and state-recall blockers are resolved as bounded host fixes (Q7); build and verify them before installing the target host. Preserve the known-good rig; install only the pinned revision plus the bounded diff, then test existing effects. Reuse the installed bundle and audit existing presets/imports first. If a bundle change is needed, back up and stage verified names/data before a new persistent scan/import; reconcile any previously imported labels without discarding user presets. Qualify basic import/recall before dual-rig performance tests. Recover by restoring the previous package/bundle and consistent state backup, then verifying the old rig; do not assume restoring `/var/pipedal` alone rolls back executables.

The approved corrected build passed its gate, and this initial-deployment command was executed after staging `install.py` **and** sibling `gig_rig.py`, verifying the effective service preset root and exact digests:


**Initial deployment complete; do not blindly rerun:** targets now exist and the initial installer deliberately refuses them. Service startup, original “Drop - Bogner” board and enabled MIDI configuration were preserved. Corrected plugin control readback passes the running-service smoke test; playable gig qualification is still pending.

The installer is initial-deployment only and refuses existing/redirected targets or unexpected settings in preset sources. It stages/validates complete trees before apt, stops the daemon before and after package scripts (scripts can briefly restart it), then promotes bundle/presets individually atomically and starts the service. Two promotions are not one transaction. Failure retains artifacts and leaves the daemon stopped; it performs no automatic rollback. Startup success alone does not verify audio. Preserve app-user data and existing PiPedal presets/imports.

The following recovery sequence was technically exercised in both directions (details above); **physical playable verification remains pending**:

1. Retain current state/new artifacts privately, verify baseline/package digests, stop both `pipedald.service` and `pipedaladmind.service`, verify inactive/PID 0 and retain their stopped state.
2. Install the exact retained previous package: `sudo apt-get install -y --allow-downgrades --reinstall /home/nam/.local/state/pipedal-gig-rig/baseline-20261004/pipedal_2.0.110_arm64.deb`; stop both services again after package scripts.
3. Quarantine post-baseline bundle/preset/config additions. Inspect archive paths/link targets and restore the consistent baseline with original ownership, ACLs and xattrs, using GNU tar into reviewed locations. An overlay alone leaves new files behind; replacing the whole store blindly can lose subsequent changes.
4. Restore/carry forward Rix's later MIDI configuration deliberately, run `sudo systemctl daemon-reload`, start `pipedald.service`, then verify original bank/preset, controls, actual audio and Captain connection.
5. Record the complete trial and playable outcome before checking [FR-10]/[5.3]. The executed technical drill does not satisfy the separate physical playable criterion.

`backup.py stage-restore` is a separate helper for its own manifest format: it verifies/extracts into a fresh staging directory and preserves POSIX owners/modes, but not ACLs/xattrs; it neither applies this GNU-tar baseline nor rolls back installed packages. Do not interchange these formats or claim a live restore from unit tests.

### [DES-TOUCH-EDITOR] Keyboard-free mobile numeric adjustment

Owner approved the proposed touch editor with “proceed”; this and standing delegated approval authorize ordered Spec → Design → Tasks review/reapproval and static rollout. Preserve the existing native/UI mutation boundaries and unstaged source. A shared compact PiPedal-styled bottom DialogEx is triggered by a button/readout for T3K numeric controls on phone/coarse-pointer or narrow layouts. No initial text input focus/keyboard; optional Enter value explicitly reveals entry. Keep desktop native entry and non-T3K controls intact. Large relative-drag horizontal slider, current physical-unit value, 44px minus/plus, Restore starting value and Done. A track touch starts from the current value rather than jumping. Starting value means the editor-opening value, not factory default. External authoritative values update idle editors; selection/connection/control changes close/reset ownership so Restore cannot target a different rig. Pointer cancellation cancels queued updates and restores only the current gesture where ownership remains valid. Closing/unmounting clears scheduled previews; release flushes the final value once.

Use production UiControl valueToRange/rangeToValue and presentPatchNumber/rawPatchNumber to preserve exact T3K physical/raw conversions and discovery contracts. Add explicit per-control fine steps; pitch Step selects integer/fractional steps, signed gain/time/pan centres remain meaningful, frequency uses verified logarithmic geometry, pitchTonality uses the verified native KnobScale::tonalityHz logarithmic1000×20^range map, reserving20kHz as Off. Embedded block frequencies20–20000Hz use logarithmic geometry, dB and mix linear; Q uses positive logarithmic geometry. Finite bounds, valid values and quantization are checked before writes. Fixed-choice controls (pitch window/buffer, oversampling, NAM size) stay selects; no continuous slider pretending intermediate values are valid.

Runtime path distinction: discovered T3K parameters here are Patch properties, whose existing onPreviewChange is deliberately empty. Therefore the touch preview must call their actual guarded setPatchValue (physical adapter → rawPatchNumber → onRawSet coupled handler or setPatchProperty → native Patch Set), coalesced to at most30Hz for ordinary live Patch values. Processing-policy/calibration controls and native coupled pan are release-only: calibration emits a full board replacement before its Patch Set and coupled native gestures reject concurrent work. Preserve those native contracts, label release behavior honestly and close on full graph replacement. Expensive controls also send only release. Do not incorrectly use PiPedal's ordinary-port preview alone and claim audible live adjustment. Ordinary live-port numeric controls, where opted in, use existing preview then final onChange. Ensure each dispatch checks Ready connection, matching bank/preset/snapshot/instance/property and editor generation; lifecycle subscription closes on changed ownership. Same-rig MIDI/readback updates accepted while idle without overwriting active pointer values. Pitch-step dependent increments read actual native patch values; avoid inferred rounding from label alone. Touch remains disabled until a finite in-range native readback is received for the current property/selection/connection; property monitors/initial reads and pitchStep reads are invalidated synchronously and refreshed on ownership/full-board changes. No guarantee of click-free audio parameter smoothing beyond native behavior. Browser guards prevent stale queued callbacks, but the existing generic Patch Set transport has no atomic server preset-identity token: a MIDI switch between local dispatch and server receipt remains a pre-existing boundary, distinct from Stage revision-validated pan. No new native API is introduced in this presentation slice. Current-owner ordinary Patch write failures surface a plain alert and read back the actual value; obsolete failures remain ignored and coupled handlers retain their existing alerts.

Embedded block and current-rig/global-profile NumberFields edit only existing local drafts. Touch movement never calls opaque-state Apply; visible Apply/save remain explicit. Shared draft editor preserves its context and invalid input text fallback, and cancels on Refresh/replacement, fieldset disabled, tab changes or RigPanel ownership changes. No native reload during slider drag, no auto-Save or T3K writeback. Soundcheck trim/measurement fields remain unchanged in this first slice unless separately mapped; continuous editing must never initiate measurement/persist trim implicitly.

### [DES-MIX-MATCH] Experimental effects between separate T3K amp and cab

At 2026-10-08T01:17:28.000Z, Rix requested this addition and authorized work while testing the existing UI today. The requested scope is recorded and reviewed in Spec → Design → Tasks order under the existing delegation to carry out routine work; all three remain Approved. This authorizes an isolated prototype, with no unqualified DSP changes to the current Rx performance rig.

**Source findings:** the pinned T3K v0.0.12 `plugin/src/Processor.cpp:25` exposes only whole-plugin input/output buses; `:1193` implements INSERT as pass-through. The embedded PiPedal chain drawing is read-only and cannot become a real effect socket. PiPedal's existing `src/Lv2Pedalboard.cpp:151` and `:299` route real host instances serially. Use two actual T3K instances: **T3K amp → ordinary PiPedal effects → T3K cab**, rather than pretending an effect was inserted into one plugin. No T3K LV2 ABI or real-time host scheduling change is included.

Rix subsequently clarified that baked amp+cab captures must stay intact; effects around such a capture continue through ordinary PiPedal routing. No decomposition is attempted.

**First source-only prototype:** extend the existing bounded `Tone3000Preset` codec and its CLI with a pure partition operation. Require an explicit amp-head confirmation; accept exactly one enabled NAM followed by one enabled, short mono cab IR on a single serial lane, with only empty insert slots elsewhere. Reject combined Amp+Cab/full-rig captures, unknown gear, disabled retained blocks, additional NAM/IR blocks, branches/dual/stereo routing, missing/invalid active assets and source oversampling or spatial processing. Source input mode must already be explicitly Left or Right; do not silently change Stereo/dual folding. Require neutral chain pan/invert/solo/output-balance controls. Inspect actual cached RIFF data for mono/short cab eligibility; metadata alone cannot prove asset compatibility or sound readiness. Keep each retained ChainBlock, EQ and all of its cached variants byte-for-byte; construct independently schema-valid complete states, retaining only that stage's relevant cache, clearing native MIDI mappings and assigning distinct derived identities. Return both states only after both validate. On any failure produce no partial output and leave the input untouched. Do not add a live host RPC, auto-load, bank mutation or ordinary Library import mapping at this step.

**DSP policy:** retain input gain, gate, pitch and NAM calibration at the amp stage; retain global tone and output level after the cab stage. Neutralize duplicated controls, chain pan/invert/solo and spatial processing; choose explicit mono input routing for the prototype, neutral cab input/calibration, and OS off for both. Retain per-block gains/mix/EQ/normalization/A2 request exactly. Source `Processor.cpp:1281` applies NAM calibration, `:1331` changes calibrated handoff when another NAM follows, `:1406` normalizes IR energy and `:1442` applies the short-IR wet pad. Therefore multi-NAM splitting is initially refused. Every instance still adds its own compulsory DC filter (`:1991`), sample-rate boundaries and workers; even a zero-effect split is not claimed identical. Initial actual-device qualification is mono at48kHz with oversampling off; stereo effects and non-48kHz/oversampled operation require further explicit tests. Do not quietly downgrade an existing oversampled rig.

**Next authoring/host step:** append a new experimental preset using two unique real host IDs, each with follow-defaults disabled and separate derived provenance. Neither derived stage may inherit ordinary `tone3000SourceKey`/`tone3000SourceHash`, because Library Update replaces an entire instance (`src/Tone3000Library.cpp:397`). Re-create explicitly from source rather than treating a segment as a full imported rig. Keep source bytes, original bank/order/IDs/output trims and all existing Rx presets unchanged; create without selecting and load only by an explicit action with current dirty/owner/readiness guards. Save and recall both instances and middle effects through existing full-state lifecycle; no deletions/reordering of the original T3K source. Display real host effects between stages using stock PiPedal chain nodes.

**Acceptance gates:** source codec malformed-input/asset/partition/immutability/round-trip checks first. Before performance availability, verify all blocks actually load offline; compare original versus zero-FX split with one DI phrase for gain/residual/latency, then verify delay placement and tails; measure finite output/clipping/CPU/xruns on the target Pi; test Save/recall/reboot/PC/snapshots and ordinary Library Update isolation; verify original Rx hashes/order and exact owner restoration. Preserve failed evidence and refuse a partial bank. Listening, physical footswitches and performance stability remain Human gates. The feature is experimental until these checks pass; today's stable UI deployment remains independent.

**Offline prototype result at 2026-10-08T01:37:47.000Z:** commit `fe59844` implements `Tone3000Preset::PartitionAmpCab(state, ampBlockId, cabBlockId, confirmAmpHead)` returning `ampState`, `cabState` and retained block IDs. The pure path validates source framing/schema, explicit identities/routing/gear/processing, active NAM JSON and short mono RIFF assets, copies retained raw block/EQ/all cache variants, seeds all43 pinned parameters with the stage policy, clears MIDI mappings, derives distinct stage identities and validates both complete StateString-only results before returning. Derived source names/IDs are explicitly length-bounded without splitting UTF-8. NAM capture classification is trimmed/case-normalized and explicit conflicting combined-capture metadata refuses partition. WAV validation requires consistent RIFF/chunk/padding/frame/rate layout, supported PCM16/24/32 or float32/64, mono/nonempty/at most1second; finite float32-representable samples must remain nonzero after actual float conversion. This is a deliberate prototype subset, not T3K's universal cab rule or an engine-readiness assertion.

CLI: `tone3000presettool partition AMP_BLOCK_ID CAB_BLOCK_ID --confirm-amp-head` accepts one bounded plugin-state JSON on stdin and buffers one complete paired JSON result; no file/host/bank publication. Actual compiled CLI checks passed for the qualifying Linear NAM/impulse-cab fixture, independent stage revalidation, both native preset exports/reimports and unchanged source hash. Missing confirmation, wrong IDs, extra args, trailing JSON, malformed JSON and empty state each exit nonzero with empty stdout. Full actual codec **ASan/UBSan1645 assertions/23 cases pass**, including132 assertions/three partition cases. Private Mac evidence: `/private/tmp/pipedal-partition-20261008/` (codec-tests, codec-tests.log, source-state.json, tone3000presettool, cli-build.log, cli-checks.json). Codec tests use the existing isolated real JSON/state-serialization/Catch harness; local CLI compilation additionally strips unused host paths and uses macOS compatibility defines for unused Linux filesystem methods. This does not qualify Linux file operations, native model loading, live audio or the actual Pi dual-stage graph.

[7.33] is complete; [7.34] remains pending. No live host RPC/UI, bank authoring or native/plugin deployment was added for this prototype. Do not drop the Outboard or downgrade owner processing to manufacture eligibility. Broader multi-NAM/stereo/oversampled arrangements need their stated measured design/qualification work.

Add a bounded authoring path with its own failure checks. |

**Recommended first release shape.** Opt-in creation of a new saved PiPedal experimental copy from an eligible saved rig. Use the exact saved bank/preset/T3K instance and full saved-board revision so the user's latest saved tweaks are the input; unapplied or unsaved current edits are never implicitly consumed. Initially accept one actual T3K instance at the top level with exactly one separate NAM amp followed by one mono cab IR, as [7.33] already requires. Source snapshots carrying values and nested/branched host placement are refused for the first authoring slice with a clear explanation rather than guessed remapping. Existing ordinary serial effects before/after the source T3K and host input/output volumes are copied once into the new board. Embedded block order/identities/settings stay governed by T3K; the two parent plugins and any PiPedal middle effects are real host nodes in the copy.

Proposed phone flow: saved-copy Preview → **Create mix & match copy** → show amp/cab names, eligibility, retained processing and destination → explicit create → receipt with bank/preset identity → separate **Load copy**. After loading, the normal PiPedal add-effect action places effects between the Amp and Cab parent nodes. Reuse stock chain, menus, controls and Apply/Save; expose concise Amp stage/Cab stage labels. Create should not choose effects or start audio itself. Unsupported sources show a specific reason, including baked/unknown capture classification, more than one NAM, Stereo/dual routing or source OS/spatial processing. These are restrictions of the first experimental implementation, not claims that T3K cannot support those configurations. T3K catalog/capture metadata and explicit amp-head confirmation do not mathematically prove that a capture contains no cabinet; unknown or conflicting metadata must be refused.

#### Dedicated pitch LV2 candidate — feasibility review only

At 2026-10-08T02:03:08.000Z, Rix asks whether the T3K pitch shifter could become an independent stackable LV2. This is a separately buildable plugin candidate, not an implemented or qualified extension of [7.34]. The existing mix-and-match policy continues to retain pitch at the Amp stage; no automatic pitch migration or owner-rig edit is authorized by this feasibility record. Refining/approving a dedicated plugin requirement, host/UI contract and tasks precedes its implementation.

**Verified source facts:** pinned v0.0.12 has a separate `plugin/include/PitchShift.h` and `plugin/src/PitchShift.cpp`. Header dependency is JUCE audio_basics; implementation adds JUCE dsp and standard C++ facilities. Neither directly depends on NAM, IR loading, the T3K catalog, account data or preset-library services. Its public surface is prepare/setEnabled/isRunning/setParams/process plus latency helpers. It processes up to two channels with per-channel audio rings and a shared control/tap path. `Processor.cpp:1152` performs Step rounding and converts Tonality20kHz to the engine's0/off value; those mappings live outside the class and must be retained by a wrapper. Controls are Enabled, Semitones±24, Step, Tonality1k–20kHz/top=off, Buffer20/30/40/60ms with30ms default. The shipped engine is the correlation-spliced delay-line implementation, not the earlier Signalsmith-based contribution (`README.md:470`).

**Latency/bypass:** `PitchShift.h` reports `(2ms + chosen buffer)/2`, giving11/16/21/31ms; 30ms at48kHz reports768samples. This is the engine's configured reporting convention, not measured end-to-end interface latency or a constant delay for every transient. The source re-syncs attacks and continuously varies the tap for non-unity ratios. Powered-on0semitones still runs a delayed path; use Enabled/off for dry operation. Power transitions blend, and processing stops after fade-out lands. A dedicated wrapper must copy those lifecycle rules and publish its own LV2 latency output; it must not assume PiPedal compensates arbitrary parallel paths. Multiple serial instances require CPU, actual delay and quality checks; their configured reported pitch latencies add, and inverse shifts do not promise exact signal recovery.

**Recommended candidate:** new independently named/identified LV2 bundle, minimal standard audio/control/latency ports and PiPedal's stock controls, pinned original DSP with preserved source hashes/notices rather than an algorithm rewrite. Build only the pitch DSP and required JUCE modules, omitting T3K's NAM/IR engines, networking and UI. Keep existing full T3K LV2 and all saved rigs installed and unchanged. Mono/stereo behavior, parameter validation, save/recall, bypass, buffer-change latency and ordinary MIDI mappings require actual host checks. Use a separate experimental preset; when explicitly moving an existing pitch setting into the new node, disable the corresponding pitch in that copy's Amp stage to avoid accidental double shifting. Serial stacking is distinct from a parallel harmony/dry blend, which needs its own routing/delay tests.

**Qualification before deployment:** compare wrapper output directly against the pinned PitchShift class under identical input, prepare/parameter order and blocks; separately compare against a neutral T3K processor, accounting for its input routing, gain/gate and compulsory post-chain DC filter. Reuse upstream tests as starting evidence, then verify LV2 lifecycle, invalid/nonfinite controls, mono/stereo, small/variable blocks, rates/windows, power/Step/tonality changes, two independent instances, save/recall and actual Pi CPU/xruns/listening. The historical header CPU estimates are not a benchmark of the new Pi plugin. An empty-chain pitch-only full T3K instance is a possible earlier diagnostic, but still carries full-processor filtering/rate-boundary/other processing and is not an isolated pitch plugin.

**Provenance/licensing:** pinned root LICENSE is MIT, copyright2026TONE3000; preserve the notice and upstream credits. JUCE is separately licensed: keep its pinned source/module licence texts and select a supported licence/build distribution path before shipping the new bundle; T3K's MIT notice does not relicense JUCE. No external branding permission or public distribution claim is inferred. Reviewed source hashes: `PitchShift.h` 6dfbd00a6c74b0020ba72e6a5f58c0b98d2c98f4c7975a7fcb9d1be6c36d0c30; `PitchShift.cpp` 069b6c62b96063c04d8fa574c7c8e5e249801acc65fb5e600eefe96194f63e3a. The source/public [PitchShift header](https://github.com/tone-3000/tone3000-plugin/blob/main/plugin/include/PitchShift.h), [T3K licence](https://github.com/tone-3000/tone3000-plugin/blob/main/LICENSE) and [JUCE9 terms](https://juce.com/legal/juce-9-licence/) support the dependency/control/licence review; the pinned local files, not future main changes, define the candidate. No source edits, new build/test result, device action or plugin deployment was performed in this feasibility turn.

### [DES-PITCH-LV2] Approved independent pitch and three-stage authoring contract

At 2026-10-08T02:09:47.000Z, Rix explicitly instructs implementation of T3K Pitch, Amp and Cab as planned, with thorough checks. [FR-27] extends the previously feasibility-only candidate; Spec → Design → Tasks reviewed and approved in that order under this explicit instruction and existing delegated approval. Implementation uses afx-task through the sprint execution contract. Three agents total maximum: root integrates frontend/build/device qualification; host_fixes owns native authoring/storage/provenance; gig_rig_tools owns independent pitch bundle/DSP tests. No broad change to existing T3K processing or active owner presets.

**Pitch bundle:** `tools/gig-rig/pitch-lv2/`, dedicated minimal C ABI LV2 wrapper plus unchanged pinned v0.0.12 PitchShift.h/.cpp, standard control/audio ports and latency output. Mono URI `https://github.com/rixrix/pipedal/plugins/t3k-pitch#mono`, stereo URI `https://github.com/rixrix/pipedal/plugins/t3k-pitch#stereo`; common port indices0enabled(default0),1semitones±24(default0),2step(default1),3tonality1000–20000Hz(default20000/off),4windowindex0–3(default1;20/30/40/60ms),5latencyoutput samples,6input_mode(default0Stereo;1Left/2Right),7inputL,8outputL; stereo additionally9inputR/10outputR. Retain exact Step rounding and Tonality-top-to-engine0 mapping, engine fade/run lifecycle, reported latency0whenrequestedoff and engine mean-delay convention whenon. Initial sample rate44.1–192kHz; refuse invalid rates rather than violating JUCE Nyquist/filter assertions. Instantiate preallocates bounded scratch/engine storage, uses optional maxBlockLength with bounded4096fallback/max65536, and chunks excess blocks without allocation in run. Null/invalid/nonfinite controls have deterministic safe values; no audio-thread allocation/locks/files/logging. Mono/stereo and activation/reactivation/save/recall/MIDI/control/latency checks are mandatory. JUCE9.0.3 modules-only build is separately reproducible/offline when dependency checkout exists; preserve original MIT/credits and JUCE licence. Add optional host packaging integration gated on an explicit dependency path; do not silently fetch or rebuild the full T3K app.

**Saved-copy native contracts:** `getTone3000MixMatchPreview` and `createTone3000MixMatch` accept exact saved `bankId,presetId,instanceId,expectedRevision` (whole saved-board hash), explicit `ampBlockId,cabBlockId,name,confirmAmpHead,includePitch` (defaulttrue), plus `creationId` canonical UUID for Create. Preview can omit creationId and confirmation; it validates eligible partition with internal confirmation solely to report complete summaries and `requiresAmpHeadConfirmation:true`, never publishes. Create requires the user's true amp-head confirmation, matching revision, both valid states and installed plugins. Returned Preview contains `sourceRevision,sourceName,name,ampBlockId,cabBlockId,ampSummary,cabSummary,includePitch,pitchControls,items,warnings,qualified:false`; no state/model bytes. Create returns `bankId,presetId,name,ampInstanceId,cabInstanceId,pitchInstanceId`(-1ifomitted), `sourceRevision,qualified:false`.

Use existing read-only Performance source list to obtain whole-board revisions and saved-target Library Preview for block identities. Pure authoring copies only a serial source board containing one enabled top-level T3K, rejects splits/nested/sidechain placement and snapshots carrying any values, preserves surrounding host effects and input/output trims once, and allocates collision-free IDs beyond existing IDs. Two codec support methods may expose one validated pinned43parameter by ID and construct a validated state with only pitchEnabled disabled; no generic arbitrary binary editing or new live Patch setter. Graph whenincludePitch: Pitch → Amp → Cab, with stock effects insertable between real nodes.

**Pitch migration ordering:** original T3K processes input gain → gate → pitch → NAM. Moving active pitch before the Amp's retained gain/gate changes that order. Therefore moving source pitch whenenabled requires sourceinputLevel=.5(unity) and gateEnabledoff; otherwise refuse with separate-test-copy guidance, never change owner processing. Sourcepitchoff yields an off Pitch node. Copy the five original pitch controls plus explicit source Left/Right input selection into the independent node and disable pitch in derived Amp only whenincluded; without Pitch keep currentpartition policy. Cab pitch/gate/calibration remain neutral. This limited eligibility is explicit, not a universal statement about pitch/gate equivalence. Original T3K selects and duplicates the source Left/Right channel before its shared pitch detector; the new node must fold that channel before DSP, even with distinct/antiphase inputs or stereo effects before it. Standalone input_mode defaults to Stereo; authoring copies Left/Right explicitly.

**Provenance/default/update protection:** optional persistent `tone3000DerivedSource` object on each derived item: `bankId,presetId,instanceId,revision,ampBlockId,cabBlockId,stage`(amp/cab/pitch), `creationId,requestHash`. Clear ordinary Library sourceKey/sourceHash/MIDI mappings on derived stages; followDefaults false and server-enforced for derived T3K on load/edit/import. Retain metadata through native/TS board serialization and clones, Save, copies and bundles. Ordinary source Update cannot overwrite a derived stage. User role labels separate Amp/Cab controls, output-matching tap/trim belongs to Cab, and wholemixed-rig export uses PiPedal bundles; T3K-exported segments are visibly labelled as stages.

**Publication/idempotency:** new `Storage::SaveTone3000MixMatchBank` writes one new `TONE3000 Experiments - ` bank with unique name using the existing bank-first/index-last private-backed publication primitive, not its four-slot entry point. Serialize creation under model lock and freshly validate saved revision. Canonical full-request hash excludes only transport details; persisted creationId+requestHash allows a same-request reconciliation to return existing identities without a new write, even if source has since changed. Different request sharing ID refuses. Scan all bounded indexed banks for receipts, including experiment banks renamed by the owner and verify matching stage IDs/count/roles; never accept partial/mismatched receipts. An exception during experimental bank/index publication latches authoring off in that Storage process until a service restart reloads the disk index; it cannot blindly reuse a stale in-memory index after failed rollback. A create outcome uncertain after disconnect is reconciled via a read-only receipt lookup or idempotent exact request; UI never automatically replays mutation. Preview/create never select a bank, capture the live plugin, change audio/defaults, save owner drafts or restart the daemon. Guard separate explicit Load with current ownership/dirty/readiness and preserve operation uncertainty on dialog dismissal.

**Qualification and rollout:** all [DES-MIX-MATCH] audio/persistence gates apply, plus direct upstream PitchShift wrapper comparison and independent/two-pitch checks. Prefer a small real saved amp+cab fixture without OS/spatial/gate/input-gain restrictions; it is a disposable test source, not an Rx replacement. Private pre-install saved/current/audio/defaults/controller/artifact backups and exact owner restore checks precede native deployment. Stop the running service for a consistent backup and preserve unsaved owner state explicitly; never assume clean. Native changes require candidate Linux build/tests and package/rollback checks before installation. Verify actual models load, gap/latency/finite output and CPU/xruns via controlled diagnostics before normal experiment availability; human actual playing/phone/MIDI/rehearsal gates remain unchecked.16×6 remains stress testing; qualification uses a recorded sensible performance configuration without quietly changing owner audio. Record any unmet or blocked gate accurately. Surgical commits separate the pitch source, native authoring, frontend, and qualification/docs.

**Delivered behavior:** `T3K Pitch (Mono)` and `T3K Pitch (Stereo)` are independent LV2 effects; the full TONE3000 plugin is unchanged. Library saved-copy rows offer **Mix & Match → Review → Create**, with explicit amp-head confirmation and a separate guarded **Load** after creation. The experimental saved board contains real Pitch → TONE3000 Amp → TONE3000 Cab instances. Ordinary PiPedal effects can be inserted before, between and after those nodes. The initial strict mono, one-NAM-plus-one-IR, non-oversampled/non-spatial eligibility and active-pitch unity-input/gate-off restrictions remain enforced; current multi-NAM/stereo/oversampled Rx rigs must not be simplified to force acceptance.

**Safety corrections:** derived identity/roles survive native/TS cloning and bundle serialization; native defaults and ordinary Library Update cannot overwrite them. Create writes a new bank without selecting it. Bounded persisted request receipts survive bank renaming, reject conflicting UUID reuse, and reconcile exact retries without duplicate publication. New opt-in Load arguments require clean current state and exact expected bank/preset, checked atomically under the model mutex before any target selection; legacy Load behavior is unchanged without these arguments. Frontend Load uses a synchronous one-flight guard, owner/full-board checks and disconnect/uncertain-outcome handling. This is a model-observed dirty-state guard, not a guarantee against an unobserved realtime mutation or an entire A→B→A history. Standalone Pitch uses the same keyboard-free touch editor with exact URI allowlisting, nonpublishing drag preview and one owned final control publication.
